# Read-only security and data protection findings

Findings are configuration observations, not exploitation tests. Exact identifiers and complete metadata are in resource-inventory.csv; evidence IDs point to the corresponding JSON records.

## Priorities

- **Recovery controls:** 287 tables have deletion protection disabled; 284 do not report PITR enabled. Prioritise data-bearing production and protected-local tables. Table counts/sizes are service estimates, not scans. Other backup systems were not exhaustively inventoried.
- **Retention:** 139/141 log groups have no expiry. Retention choices require operational/data requirements; no deletions performed.
- **Storage exposure:** account-level S3 public-access block is absent. Public-policy buckets: projectrespawn.com. Public-policy status alone does not establish private-data exposure; no objects were listed/downloaded. Bucket controls and website config below.
- **Runtime:** 1 Lambda has a legacy runtime; see identifiers below. Runtime support dates are sourced from [AWS Lambda runtimes](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/lambda/latest/dg/lambda-runtimes.html). No runtime changes performed.
- **IAM:** 946 inline Allow statements contain wildcard actions/resources. Many are expected generated resource-prefix scopes or services requiring wildcard resources; they need contextual review, not blanket removal. Managed policies and trust documents are recorded in iamAuthorization evidence.
- **Monitoring:** 0 existing Access Analyzers in eu-north-1; none enabled by this audit.

## Ownership and attachment review

IAM roles are matched to CloudFormation, Lambda roles, AppSync data-source roles and identity-pool roles. RoleLastUsed was retrieved for Lambda execution roles; IAM last-use data and attached policies do not prove all runtime consumers. Roles without a mapped workload remain UNKNOWN. Resource tags are recorded separately from inherited stack tags; missing project/environment tags reduce confidence.

Node.js 18 was deprecated on 1 September 2025 according to the linked AWS runtime documentation. RavensBot ownership is not established by naming alone; confirm its owner before proposing an upgrade.

The public projectrespawn.com bucket has index/error website configuration and is referenced by a disabled CloudFront distribution. This supports a historical website interpretation, not a conclusion of exposed private uploads. DNS/Amplify domain associations and ownership must be checked before any change.
